Reporting this sub-check as FAIL.
5. Dev server restart (kill + npm run dev again): client/projects/events all still present via API - PASS.
6. Project settings: all three Bloomreach identifiers (token, API base URL, project URL) saved and survived a reload (after reselecting the project, see #2) - PASS.
7. File store: backend upload+retrieve works and matches /api/projects/<id>/files/... (verified directly), but the only UI path to attach an image (event trigger) is unusable - see repro #3, FAIL.
8. Stubbed areas: Journeys shows Unavailable until T-1-03, no network call, no console error - correct. Catalogs/Sources/Properties/Events are fully functional (not stubs, as expected - T-1-02 rebuilt them). Documentation is a static legacy content page (untouched by T-1-02 per handoff), loads fine but 404s on /branding/agency-logo.png (missing base-path prefix, pre-existing, not in scope) - PASS overall for this scenario.

Delete a client with projects: backend returns 409 with error text "Client cannot be deleted because it owns 2 project(s)."; UI code path (handleDelete -> onDelete -> formError render) correctly surfaces it instead of crashing. Could not click through the native window.confirm dialog with the browser tool, so this was verified by direct API call plus code read of src/pages/Clients.tsx lines 118-127 and src/features/clients/hooks/useClients.ts lines 88-95 - PASS.
10. Network: zero supabase requests all session; grep -ri supabase src/ returns nothing - PASS.

Failures (numbered repro, expected vs actual):

1. Clients page is unreachable from a fresh install, deadlocking client/project creation.
   Steps: (a) fresh DB, load app, see Project required gate with message Create a client and a project from the project switcher. (b) Click Clients in the sidebar. (c) Open header Project switcher, click Create New Project - modal says No clients yet, create one from the Clients page first.
   Expected: clicking Clients opens the Clients page (per the implementer own handoff verification step 3: Sidebar -> Clients -> Add client).
   Actual: main content still renders the Project required gate; get_page_text after the click still shows Project required / No project is available. There is no way to reach Clients with zero projects.
   Cause: src/components/layout/Layout.tsx lines 206-247 - mainContent short-circuits to the gate whenever hasValidProjectContext is false, for every activeTab including clients and settings. hasValidProjectContext (src/features/projects/context/ProjectShellContext.tsx lines 52-79) is false whenever projects.length is 0, regardless of whether any clients exist. Since no project can be created without a client, and no client can be created without visiting the gated Clients page, this is a hard deadlock on any fresh database.
   Worked around by POST /api/clients directly to continue QA; once a client existed and a project was created through the modal (project creation itself worked fine), the Clients tab became reachable and worked correctly (add/edit persisted).

2. A real page reload loses the active project and does not restore it from the URL.
   Steps: select Project A (URL correctly becomes .../tracking-plan/p/project-a/events), then reload the page (window.location.reload(), equivalent to F5).
   Expected (qa-roundtrip scenario 4 / implementer verification step 6): reload shows the same events/properties without extra steps.
   Actual: main content shows Project required - Select a project from the header menu to open its tracking plan.; the URL is silently rewritten to .../tracking-plan/events (project segment stripped). The underlying data is intact - confirmed via GET /api/projects/:id/events and by reselecting Project A from the header dropdown, which shows the same event/property/source data - but the session itself does not survive a reload.
   Cause: src/store.tsx lines 94 and 125 - activeProjectId is initialized to a hardcoded placeholder UUID (00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001), not read from the URL or any persisted storage (no zustand persist middleware, no localStorage). Layout.tsx effects only ever write the /p/:key URL segment from store state (pushPath, lines 175-204); nothing reads /p/:key from the URL on mount and calls setActiveProject. The drop invalid active ids effect (Layout.tsx lines 148-160) then treats the placeholder id as invalid, clears it, and strips the URL.

3. Event triggers (and therefore any uploaded trigger image) are silently dropped on save - never persisted on create or update.
   Steps: open event purchase, go to Triggers, click Add trigger, type a title, click Save Event. Reopen the event, or GET /api/projects/:id/events.
   Expected: the trigger persists so it shows on reopen, and (per the handoff own verification step 8) an uploaded trigger image renders after reload.
   Actual: triggers_json is always []. Network capture shows the PATCH .../events/:id request the frontend sends (src/pages/Events.tsx lines 299-304, body triggers_json: [...]) but the JSON response for that same request already comes back with triggers_json: [].
   Cause (confirmed by reading all three write-path layers): src/backend/routes/events.ts lines 20-42 (inputFromBody) never reads body.triggers_json, so it never reaches the DAL; src/backend/dal/event.dal.ts EventInput type (lines 57-77) has no triggers_json field; updateEvent patch builder (lines 238-260) never sets it even if present; createEvent INSERT (lines 179-190) hardcodes triggers_json to NULL unconditionally. Triggers can never be saved through any code path today.
   Note: the FileStore backend itself is fine in isolation - a direct multipart POST /api/projects/:id/files?kind=trigger returned 201 with path under <projectId>/trigger/..., and GET /api/projects/:id/files/<path> served it back with the correct content-type, matching the required /api/projects/<id>/files/ URL shape. The bug is specifically that the trigger row holding the image_path reference can never be saved, so the file-store feature is unreachable end-to-end via the UI.

- Vite HMR WebSocket connection to ws://127.0.0.1:3000/tracking-plan/ failed errors appear in the console starting after the dev-server restart (scenario 5) and on later reloads; the very first clean boot (scenario 1) had zero console errors. Dev-only noise, likely a base-path/proxy quirk, not a production concern, but technically shows up as console errors on a later reload.
- src/pages/Documentation.tsx requests /branding/agency-logo.png without the Vite base path, producing a console 404; file is explicitly untouched and out of scope for T-1-02 per the implementer own handoff notes (T-1-03 job).

- Two PATCH requests fire to /api/projects/:id/events/:id for a single Save Event click (one from handleSave main body, one immediately after for triggers_json) - harmless and idempotent here but worth a look.
- E3_LOCAL_USER_NAME / git user.name / Local user fallback chain (src/backend/middleware/auth.ts lines 34-50) matches the D-25 Q8 spec by code read; not exercised live since nothing in the UI surfaces the resolved name yet.

1. WARNING (not one of the required literal scenario-2 steps, but a direct regression against the implementer's claimed fix for finding #3, found by prioritising it in-browser as requested): opening a project URL that differs from the last-active project stored in localStorage silently discards the URL and loads the wrong project instead.
   Steps: (a) select Project A via the switcher (URL becomes .../p/project-a/events). (b) Navigate directly (fresh page load, e.g. a bookmark or shared link, or the browser tool's navigate) to .../p/project-b/events while Project A is the last one stored in localStorage.
   Expected (implementer HANDOFF #3): resolves /p/:key against the loaded project list on mount/popstate (URL wins), falling back to the localStorage value otherwise.
   Actual: the app loads Project A (from localStorage) and rewrites the URL back to /p/project-a/events, discarding the requested project-b entirely. Reproduced 3 times, both directions (A->B URL and B->A URL), 100% reproducible.
   Cause: src/components/layout/Layout.tsx:153-180 (URL -> store sync) is gated on !isLoading, which is true until the async project-list fetch resolves, so on first mount it does nothing. Meanwhile src/components/layout/Layout.tsx:182-190 (store -> URL sync) has no such gate and runs as soon as activeProjectKey has any value - which it already does on the very first render, since store.tsx now restores it from localStorage synchronously at store-creation time (finding #3's own fix). That second effect wins the race and pushes the URL to match localStorage before the first effect ever gets a chance to read the original URL. Net effect: localStorage wins over the URL, the opposite of the documented intent. Does not affect same-URL reload or root-URL restore, which is why the three literal scenario-2 checks still pass.

2. FAIL: creating a catalog field with a name that already exists in the catalog fails silently in the UI, even though the backend returns a clean 409 (repeat of the reviewer's previous FAIL round finding #3c, not addressed in this fix round).

   Actual: network log shows POST .../catalogs/:id/fields -> 409 Conflict (correct, no 500), but the UI shows absolutely nothing - no error text, no toast, the typed name just sits in the box unchanged, field list unchanged. Confirmed via screenshot and DOM inspection.
   Cause: src/pages/Catalogs.tsx:194-205 addField() only has an if (res.ok) {...} branch with no else; there is a saveError state already wired up and rendered for the catalog-level Save action (src/pages/Catalogs.tsx:132,173,274) but addField never sets it.

3. FAIL: saving an event, a property, or a catalog with an empty name is a completely silent no-op - no network request, no error message, no disabled button, no visual feedback of any kind.
   Steps: (a) Events -> Add Event -> leave Name blank -> click Save Event. (b) Properties -> Add Property -> leave Name blank -> click Save Property. (c) Catalogs -> Add Catalog -> leave Name blank -> click Save Catalog.
   Expected (task scenario 5): expect a 400 with a visible validation message, no 500.
   Actual: in all three cases, clicking Save produces zero network requests (confirmed via network log immediately after each click) and the form just sits there unchanged - no error text, no shake, no red border, the Save button is not even disabled so it looks fully actionable. A user gets no indication anything went wrong.
   Cause: src/pages/Events.tsx:267, src/pages/Properties.tsx:176 and src/pages/Catalogs.tsx:157 all have an identical client-side guard (if name is blank or no projectId, return) at the top of handleSave, which exits before ever calling the API or setting any error state.
   Contrast: src/pages/Clients.tsx:110-116 handleSave has no such guard and always calls the API; in practice the client Save button is disabled when name/key are empty (confirmed live: disabled true), which is a defensible client-side pattern, though it likewise never reaches the documented "400 with a visible message" - it just prevents the click. Client is still the least-bad of the four.

### 2026-09-24 19:30 | qa-tester -> supervisor | PASS | T-1-04
Tested branch `task/T-1-04-property-event-model` (5622bd1) in the implementer worktree, app run with `E3_DB_PATH=data/qa-t104.sqlite E3_FILES_PATH=data/qa-t104-files npm run dev`, http://127.0.0.1:3000/tracking-plan/. Overall verdict: PASS, all scenarios pass, 119/119 unit tests pass (`npm test`), no 500s or supabase requests in the whole session, server stopped and port 3001 confirmed free at the end.

Per-scenario results:
1. Boot clean — PASS. App loaded, no console errors on first paint; created client "QA Client T104" and project "QA Project T104" via the project switcher with no errors.
2. Property editor fields — PASS. Scope, data type incl. `money` format checkbox, property type, priority (critical/high/medium/low), status (planned/implemented/ignored), allowed values editor (value/label/description/standard-event select from `bloomreach.ts`), notes all present. Created event-scope enum property `action` (add/remove/update, `add` hinted `add_to_cart`); Properties table showed badge "enum · 3". Duplicate-value validation: adding a 4th value "add" (dup) and saving showed a visible message `Duplicate allowed value "add".` and the network request returned 400 (not 500); removing the dup and re-saving succeeded.
3. Event editor fields — PASS. Created event `cart_update` with business name, type, description, purpose, page, trigger, grain, identity note, owners, priority (medium), status (implemented), expiration (enabled, 45 days), not suitable for, agent instructions, one derived metric (name/expression/description). Saved, reloaded the whole app, reopened the editor: every field (including derived metric and expiration) persisted exactly. Events table showed type "custom", status "implemented", expiration "45 days", properties count updating live (0 → 4 after linking).
4. Roles — PASS. Attached `action` as discriminator: badge "discriminator" shown next to the property name and allowed values listed inline with the `add → add_to_cart` chip (verified via screenshot/read_page). Attached second enum `channel` as discriminator: succeeded. Attempted a third discriminator (`os`): save was refused with a visible message "This event already has 2 discriminator properties (D-03 rule 3)." and network `PUT .../events/:id/properties` returned 400 with `{code: "MAX_DISCRIMINATORS"}` (confirmed via read_network_requests body), no 500. Attempted plain non-enum property `note` with role `context`: refused with visible message `Property "note" has no allowed values; role "context" requires an enum property.` and 400 (no code field checked on this one but message matches ROLE_REQUIRES_ENUM semantics; request logged as 400 Bad Request). Set `required`, `payload_key` override (`action_key`), and `source` (`Web`) on the `action` link; saved (200 OK); reloaded the app and reopened the editor: required checkbox, discriminator role, source "Web" and payload key override "action_key" all persisted.
5. Scope filter on Properties — PASS. "All scopes" shows 5 properties across event/customer; filtering to `customer` showed only `email`; filtering to `event` showed the 4 event-scope properties; counts updated correctly on each filter change.
6. Project settings default expiration — PASS. Settings page has a "Default event expiration" checkbox + count + units row; set to enabled/90/months, saved, reloaded the app, reopened Settings: value persisted exactly.
7. Bundles — NOT APPLICABLE (per task instructions "if the UI exists"). No bundle-creation UI exists on the Properties page or elsewhere in this branch; the property editor only renders a bundle checklist when `bundles.length > 0`, and there is no page to create one, so this could not be exercised. Not treated as a failure since T-1-04's acceptance only requires bundles to "remain functional" (a shell feature), not that bundle management UI be added in this task.
8. Regression — PASS. Reload persistence confirmed at every step above; dev server log (`npm run dev` stdout) shows zero 5xx across the whole session; `read_network_requests` for `supabase` returned no matches; console errors were only the expected 400s from the deliberate validation tests in scenarios 2 and 4, plus a pre-existing unrelated CSS warning (`var(--brand-primary)` not a valid color, from the client-primary-color Settings field, out of scope for T-1-04).

Also ran `npm test` in the worktree: 10 files / 119 tests passed, including `src/backend/dal/property-event-model.dal.test.ts` (11 tests) covering the DAL validations named in the acceptance criteria.

No exports were produced by this task (Property/Event CRUD only, no import/export surface), so no files were saved under `.claude/team/reports/qa/T-1-04/`.
- ERROR 1 src/backend/dal/event.dal.ts:222-283 createEvent and :287-343 updateEvent validate nothing but name; routes/events.ts:21-42 forwards as-is. Reproduced at DAL level: POST /api/projects/:id/events {name:'e1', status:'bogus', event_type:'weird', priority:'urgent'} -> 201 stored; {name:'e2', expiration_json:{enabled:true,count:'abc',units:'fortnights'}} -> 201; expiration_json:'yes' -> 201; derived_metrics_json:'revenue' -> 201; derived_metrics_json:[{foo:1}] -> 201; tags_json:'x' -> 201. Plan T-1-04 requires event_type, priority, status and expiration_json (enabled, count, units from the enum module) with 400 and field; the T-1-04 delta requires D-25 priority/status values. Only the UI selects guard these; the Events table then renders "abc fortnights" (Events.tsx:70-73). Needs a validateEventFields mirroring validatePropertyFields: event_type in ORIGIN_TYPES, priority in PRIORITIES, status in EVENT_STATUSES, expiration_json null or {enabled:boolean, count:positive integer, units in EXPIRATION_UNITS}, derived_metrics_json array of {name non-empty string, expression non-empty string, description string|null}, tags_json string[].
- ERROR 2 src/backend/dal/event.dal.ts:436 (attach), :504-513 (update), :579 (replace): EventProperty role is never checked against EVENT_PROPERTY_ROLES and link priority/status never against PRIORITIES/ENTITY_STATUSES. Reproduced: POST /events/:id/properties {property_id, role:'bogus', priority:'urgent', status:'done'} -> 201 with role "bogus" stored; PUT .../properties {properties:[{property_id, role:'whatever'}]} -> 200; PATCH .../properties/:pid {role:'nope'} -> 200. - ERROR 3 src/backend/dal/property.dal.ts:369-449 updateProperty accepts emptying allowed_values_json on a property currently linked with role discriminator or context, breaking domain rule 1 through the primary UI path. ### 2026-09-25 10:23 | reviewer -> supervisor | INFO | T-2-01
Incident during review cleanup, repaired: the scratch worktree's node_modules junction into the main tree was not removed before `git worktree remove --force` (the `cmd /c rmdir` call silently no-oped from the agent shell because the cwd path contains a non-ASCII character), and git on Windows recursed through the junction and deleted the main tree's `node_modules/.bin` shims (96 -> 0; all packages incl. better-sqlite3, vite, vitest, tsx, typescript stayed present; git aborted on a locked file). Repaired with an incremental `npm install` against package-lock (29 packages relinked, no version changes): 96 shims back, `vitest`/`tsx`/`vite`/`tsc`/`tsup`/`concurrently` present, top-level package count unchanged (345). Dev servers on 3000/3001 were not touched and kept running. Nothing in the repo tree changed (git status: only BOARD.md and TASKS.md). Rule for future scratch worktrees: create and remove the junction with PowerShell (`New-Item -ItemType Junction` / `[System.IO.Directory]::Delete(path)` after checking `LinkType -eq 'Junction'`), and abort `git worktree remove` if `node_modules` still exists in the worktree.

Express is single-threaded, so one `GET /api/projects/:id/export/documentation.html` hangs the whole API for every user. Input: `PATCH /use-cases/:id` body `{"narrative":"Intro\r\n\r\n## Flow\r\n\r\n1. a"}` (routes/use-cases.ts:27 stores it verbatim, the DAL only trims; no CRLF normalization anywhere on the path, and T-2-10's MCP will write narratives from agents), then download documentation.html. Fix: normalize `\r\n`/`\r` to `\n` at the top of `parseNarrativeBlocks` (and ideally on save in `use-case.dal.ts`), use the same regexes for detection and paragraph exclusion, and make the paragraph branch always consume at least one line; add a CRLF test for heading, list and paragraph.

Notable non-blocking observations (not defects): (a) the event editor's "Attach" (linked-property) control is client-side-only staging — it does not POST until "Save Event" is clicked; a `GET` immediately after clicking Attach (without Save) shows the property still unlinked. This matches the rest of the Edit Event form's save-on-submit pattern but is easy to mistake for a live save since the row appears in the UI immediately; worth a one-line note in the UI or docs. (b) `runComparison` recomputes over every non-archived `QaRun` (by design, D-21), so re-pasting a "fixed" customer-events-export after a "broken" one does not by itself resolve a finding — the earlier run must be archived first (used throughout scenarios 3/4/6/7 here, consistent with the T-2-04 QA precedent). (c) an arithmetic aggregate over a list property that is present but whose items are missing the aggregated field (e.g. `product_list[].price` absent) sums that item as 0 rather than skipping, so a rule needing that field always reports a violation until the field is actually supplied — different from the already-documented "list entirely absent -> skip" case; not something this task's fixture exercises by default (cart_update's product_list in the shared fixture never carries `price`), so the "Cart totals match the product list" template cannot be demonstrated as resolvable without adding a price value to the paste, which I did for scenario 3/4.

No defects found against the acceptance scenarios. Recommend T-2-07 -> DONE.

### 2026-09-25 17:04 | reviewer -> supervisor | FAIL | T-2-08
Reviewed task/T-2-08-event-card-document (9c28a11, f109c58, e319310, a5c6a03) against agent-setup in a scratch worktree with its own `npm ci` (no junction; main tree `node_modules/.bin` verified intact afterwards). Re-ran: `npm test` 906 passed / 78 files (matches handoff), `npx tsc --noEmit` clean, `npm run build` ok, dry-run merge onto agent-setup clean, no `.claude/team` edits in the diff. Certification rule verified per D-05/D-21/D-25 Q10 (`document.service.ts:255-271`: open findings for the event across every comparison id incl. rule findings, only `error` counts, sdk/platform `applicable:false`, `status_ignored`, observations from non-archived runs via `listObservationsInScope`, live-resolved). Codegen helper move (`codegen.service.ts:322-360`) preserves behaviour (export.service tests green) and the import-cycle claim holds (export.service imports document.service). Snapshot normalisation is deterministic (sorted keys, sequential id placeholders, `generated_at` stripped, `listEvents` ORDER BY name). HTML export reuses `buildEventCard` and escapes (`sections.ts:201`, badge reasons escaped). Findings:
1. ERROR `src/domain/document/trackingDocument.ts:1-5,253` and `docs/TRACKING-DOCUMENT.md:6-9`: D-40 Q9 ruled the tracking document carries the per-event findings summary plus the list of open findings of every severity, uncapped. The document ships only `findings_summary` counts and `EventCard.quality` counts and explicitly states "no raw ... full finding rows from MCP list_findings", i.e. the option D-40 rejected. Input: fixture project after `runComparison` -> `GET /export/tracking-document.json` has no finding rows anywhere (`grep '"kind"' snapshot` finds only rule kinds). Fix (additive, no version bump): `findings: FindingRow-like[]` at top level or `EventCard.quality.findings[]` with kind, severity, comparison, property, variant, expected, actual, detail.
2. ERROR `src/backend/services/document.service.ts:292` vs `:387`: the card's coverage summary keys context columns by property name (`propertyNameById.get(...)`) while `computeEventCoverageGrid` and the plan use the link `payload_key`. Input: context property `location` attached with `payload_key: 'loc'`, observation `cart_update {loc: 'web'}` -> card `coverage {observed_count: 0, unobserved_count: 2}`, `GET /events/:id/coverage` cells `[{value:'web', count:1}]`. Same event, two answers. Fix: build the card's context columns from `link.payload_key?.trim() || name` (share one helper with `computeEventCoverageGrid`).
3. ERROR `src/domain/document/trackingDocument.ts:111-119` / `document.service.ts:302-310`: `EventCard.rules[]` drops the rule's `variant_id`, so a variant-scoped guardrail (D-30's own example) reads as an unconditional event rule. Input: `purchase` with variant `purchase[purchase_status=refunded]`, rule `{variant_id: <that variant>, kind: bound, total_price lt 0}` -> card `rules: [{name:'refund negative', expression_text:'total_price lt 0', ...}]` with no scope; an agent (T-2-10 `get_expected_payload`) would require negative totals on every purchase. Fix: add `variant_id` and `variant_name` to `EventCardRule` and render the scope in the docs table.
4. WARNING `document.service.ts:231-237`: `expiration.source` never yields `variant` although the type and plan allow it, `Variant.expiration_json` exists, the expiration import writes it (`expirationSettingsImport.service.ts:114-120`) and the engine resolves variant first (`definitionVsObservations.ts:134`). Input: event with no `expiration_json`, variant with `{enabled:true,count:30,units:'days'}`, project default null -> card `{effective:null, source:null}` while the engine applies 30 days to that variant's observations. Fix: carry `expiration` per `variants[]` entry (additive) or document the per-variant gap.
5. WARNING `document.service.ts:259,410` and `export.service.ts:187`: every `buildEventCard` call re-lists and re-resolves all non-archived observations of the project (`resolveObservationsInScope`) plus `listEvents`, `listStandardEventMappings` and a fresh `createEventResolver`; `buildTrackingDocument` and the HTML export call it once per event, so cost is events x observations. Measured: 40 custom events x 2,000 observations -> `buildTrackingDocument` 2.5 s (single card 59 ms); 200 events x 10,000 observations extrapolates to about a minute per document fetch. Per-property work is fine (no per-property queries; `nameOf` is cached). Fix: resolve observations once per document/export and pass them (and the shared lookups) into a `buildEventCard(projectId, eventId, ctx?)` overload.
6. WARNING `trackingDocument.ts:77` / `document.service.ts:227`: `variants[].expected_payload` is a flat sample `{key: value}`; it carries the discriminator literals (verified) but no required flag per key, so an agent cannot tell required from optional keys without cross-referencing `properties[]` by `payload_key`. Suggest an additive `required_keys: string[]` on the variant (or per-key objects) before T-2-10 freezes the MCP tool output.
7. INFO `coverage.ts:103`: `context_values_json` is read by `payload_key`; the plan words it `context_values_json[property]`. Input: `context_values_json: {location:'app'}` on the `loc` payload_key link above -> not counted. Decide the key (name or payload_key) and state it in `docs/TRACKING-DOCUMENT.md` before MCP `submit_observation` writes the column.
8. INFO: sdk/platform events get the full card, not the plan's "reduced card" (superset, acceptable; docs say so implicitly). `computeEventCertification` builds the whole card incl. codegen for one boolean block (fine for now). D-34 names the contract type `TrackingDocumentV1`; the branch has `TrackingDocument` with `version: number` -- rename or alias now while there is one consumer. Download button sits on the Events page instead of the Documentation page (reasonable, the static Documentation page is the knowledge base).
9. Use case coverage: recommendation is to accept T-2-11's per-requirement `GET /use-cases/:id/coverage` shape as the documented deviation from the plan's grid union (it answers "is each requirement observed", and per-event grids are one call away), and note in the T-2-10 acceptance that MCP `get_coverage_grid(use_case)` returns that shape.
Verdict FAIL on findings 1-3. T-2-08 set back to IN_PROGRESS.

Finding 2 detail (scenario 2, the only FAIL): reviewer findings 1 and 3 are both confirmed fixed — `EventCard.findings[]` on `cart_update`/`purchase` carry full rows with exactly the 13 specified fields (id, comparison, kind, severity, property, variant, rule, message, expected, actual, qa_run_id, first_seen, last_seen), and the docs HTML has a per-event "Open findings" table (verified `documentation.html:206-217` on `Opt_in_proof`, a `missing_event` warning row). Root cause: 2 open findings have `event_id: null` and so cannot be attached to any event card — `dca944d0-...` (`definition_vs_observations`/`shape_inconsistent`, "Property \"product_ids\" is observed with inconsistent JSON types across events: array, string.") and `12377d42-...` (`mapping_vs_observations`/`rename_mismatch`, the newsletter->brand_consent rename). These are legitimately cross-event/consent-level findings, not obviously mis-scoped, but as shipped they are invisible in the tracking document entirely (not in any event's `findings[]`, not broken out elsewhere) while still counted in `findings_summary`. Fix suggestion: either surface event_id-less findings in a top-level `findings: []` alongside `findings_summary`, or explicitly document the gap and adjust the equality claim to "sum of event findings plus N cross-event/consent findings equals the Findings page open count." - ERROR 1 `src/backend/dal/change-log.dal.ts:179-182,189`: keyset paging `before=<changed_at>` uses `changed_at < ?` alone while the order is `changed_at DESC, id DESC`; rows sharing the cursor's millisecond are skipped. DAL probe: 60 `event_property` rows from one `replaceEventProperties` span 14 distinct `changed_at`; `limit=7` pages with `before` returned 46 of 60. Route probe after the four fixture imports: `GET /history?limit=25&before=<last changed_at of the page>` collected 1716 of 1746 rows (966 distinct timestamps), 30 rows never reachable. Imports write hundreds of rows per millisecond, so the History page (T-3-02) would silently lose rows. Fix: composite cursor `(changed_at, id)` — accept `before_id` (or a `<changed_at>|<id>` cursor) and filter `changed_at < ? OR (changed_at = ? AND id < ?)`; add a DAL test that pages a 60-row same-millisecond batch with `limit` smaller than the batch and asserts no gaps and no duplicates.
- WARNING 2 `src/backend/routes/history.ts:16-20` with `change-log.dal.ts:184`: `limit=1.5` passes `numberParam` (finite) and binds a REAL to `LIMIT` -> SQLite `datatype mismatch` -> 500 `INTERNAL_ERROR`. - WARNING 3 `src/backend/dal/event.dal.ts:648,658`: `deleteEvent` deletes the event's `standard_event_mappings` and `variants` rows and nulls `identity_rules.trigger_event_id` without change_log rows; only the event `delete` row is written. Probe: event with one variant and one mapping -> one row. A variant's history therefore ends in create/update and never delete, while `deleteVariant` called directly does write a delete row. Decision: does not break the phase 3 exit criterion (scenario 9 is the description edit, a link change under the event, an import batch) and does not break D-44's "no history missed by a new route" (this is DAL-internal), but it is inconsistent with the D-44 rule that every definition-mutating DAL path logs. Fix in the fix round for variants (the `variantRows` loop at :651-656 already has id and name; write one `variant/delete` row each with `parent_entity_id = eventId`); mapping and identity-rule rows go to T-3-02 when those entity types are wired — note it in the T-3-02 brief.

- INFO 6 `replaceEventProperties` calls `getPropertyById` twice per link (validation loop and the change-log loop, each with a bundle lookup); reuse the first loop's map. Not blocking.
- INFO 7 `deleteVariant` is edited by both T-3-01 (recordChange) and T-3-07 (MAPPING_IN_USE guard) with no textual conflict; after both merge, check `recordChange` still runs inside the transaction on the `force` path.
- INFO 8 Documented deviations accepted: no CHECK on `source` (open `import:<format>` prefix, matches the plan), `changed_at` is per row not per transaction (rows of one mutation can straddle a millisecond).

Express 4's default `extended` query parser turns a repeated or bracketed parameter into an array/object, `stringParam` returns `undefined` for it, and the filter is silently dropped -- the same "unfiltered 200 instead of a 400" class the QA FAIL was about. Probe: `?action=delete&action=update`, `?action[]=delete`, `?action[x]=delete`, `?entity_type[]=event`, `?limit[]=5`, `?before[]=x`, `?limit=5&limit=7` all 200 with all 68 rows (actions create/delete/update), so `parseAction`'s "400 otherwise" is bypassed by a repeated `action`. Pre-existing for the other filters, one-line fix (reject non-string values in `assertKnownParams` or make `stringParam` throw on arrays/objects for this route); fold into T-3-02, which builds the History page URLs.
- INFO 2 `src/backend/routes/history.ts:69`: `action=` (empty) is treated as "not given" (200, unfiltered), consistent with the other filters and with `limit=`; acceptable, noting it so T-3-02's UI never sends an empty `action`.
- INFO 3: the new route tests cover single-filter action, invalid action and one unknown parameter; the action + cursor combination is only covered by this probe. Not blocking.
Verdict PASS. T-3-01 stays at QA per the supervisor's ruling (no second QA run; merge directly). WARNING 1 and INFO 2 go to the T-3-02 brief.

Backup downloaded (`backup-source.json`, 131128 bytes) and restored via Clients page both ways: file-input path (client QA T-3-05 Client, name "Restored", key "fixture-copy") and paste path (key "fixture-copy-2"), both applied successfully with no warnings and matching counts (events 12, properties 24, event_properties 26, variants 2, standard_event_mappings 6, consent_categories 1, use_cases 1, requirements 5, qa_runs 1, observations 19, findings 29). Scenario 10 verified: tracking-document.json of source and restored deep-equal after masking ids/timestamps/generated_at (only `project.name`/`project.key` differ, as expected); acknowledged finding stays `status: resolved, resolved_by: "user"` under its remapped fingerprint; purchase trigger screenshot restored byte-identical (76 bytes) at the rewritten file path; comparison re-run on the restored project produces the same 29 findings (0 new, 0 resolved); source project's own findings (29, 1 resolved) unchanged after all restores. Errors: same key twice -> 409 `PROJECT_KEY_TAKEN`; editing the payload after preview -> 409 `PAYLOAD_CHANGED`; wrong `format` -> 400 `BAD_REQUEST`; Clients/projects list carries exactly the 3 expected projects afterward (source + fixture-copy + fixture-copy-2), no orphans from the three failed/duplicate attempts. History API (`GET /history`) on the restored project shows exactly one `action: restore` row with the counts diff plus the carried `purchase` trigger-upload row (T-3-02's History UI isn't in this branch's worktree, verified via API only — not a T-3-05 defect). No 500s, zero supabase requests, console errors limited to Vite HMR websocket noise (expected, `--host 0.0.0.0` workaround) plus the three errors I deliberately triggered.

FAIL reason: acceptance criterion "Apply -> the app switches to the new project" (T-3-05 acceptance and scenario 3) does not hold. `src/features/clients/components/RestoreBackupForm.tsx:97-101` calls `setActiveProject({id, key})` then `window.location.reload()` without first changing the URL. `src/components/layout/Layout.tsx:183-189` + `src/components/layout/resolveInitialProject.ts:23-25` make the `/p/:key` URL segment win over the store on every mount/reload; since the reload happens on the *old* project's URL (e.g. `/p/qa-t305-source/clients`), `resolveInitialProject` re-selects the old project on reload and silently undoes `setActiveProject`. Reproduction: Clients page (any project) -> Restore backup -> preview -> fill client/name/key -> Apply -> network shows `POST .../apply` 200 with the new project in the response -> page reloads -> project switcher still shows the previous project; the new project only exists reachable by manually selecting it from the switcher. Expected: after Apply the URL and active project both point at the newly restored project (e.g. `/p/fixture-copy/events`). Actual: URL and active project stay on the source project; the new project is otherwise fully correct (verified via direct navigation). This is the same code path flagged pre-QA ("reloads immediately on success and never shows apply warnings[]") but is a stronger break than previously described — it does not merely skip showing warnings, it fails to switch projects at all. Recommend a pre-DONE fix: navigate to `/p/<newKey>/events` (or call `pushPath`) before/instead of `window.location.reload()`, and surface `data.warnings` if the apply response ever carries any.

Also flagged (not blocking, for T-3-08 or a docs pass): Settings page's CSV row count for `change_log` reads 0 on both the source and restored projects, while the backup export's `change_log` array holds 66 entries (matching the History API) — the Settings count query looks like it undercounts/miscounts change_log rows; worth a follow-up ticket, likely T-3-02/T-3-03 territory rather than T-3-05's own code.
